The scroll panes (`.ncm-term__detail/__login/__gate` and the sidebar's `.ncm-term__list`) pair `overflow-y:auto` with `overflow-x:hidden` (the phantom-horizontal-scrollbar gotcha). **The sidebar (`.ncm-term__side`) is NOT a scroller anymore** — it's a flex column (`overflow:hidden`): account switcher + folder strip (`.ncm-term__folds`, max-height 40% w/ internal scroll) + list label stay pinned, only `.ncm-term__list` scrolls (the `_scrollMemo` targets it), and the GM New-message button pins to the bottom. **Message rows are separated cards** (border + 6px gap, priority-colored left edge, `--urgent/--critical` modifiers): line 1 = pdot + From + short date (`formatCyberDate dateOnly`), line 2 = subject, line 3 = a one-line preview — a stripped-HTML body snippet, or the file-type tag (`CONTENT_TYPE_INFO` icon+label) for typed content. Headless-verified (`sidebar1.png`).

**GM bypass is EXPLICIT, not automatic.** `requiresAuth`/`checkNetworkAccess` bypass only when `terminalState.gmBypassed` is set (via force-unlock), mirroring shard `forceDecrypt` — so a GM sees locked accounts and can test the login (or force-unlock to skip). Don't re-add an `isGM()` auto-bypass; that made every account read "open" for the GM tester. Login cards show truthful lock status.

**Machine uplink (Layer 1) — the TERMINAL's own network, never the player's.** Three layers: Layer 0 = the existing reachability gate (`config.network` — "be on network X to reach this box", player's network, unchanged); **Layer 1** = the machine's own connection (`config.connection = { interfaces: [{networkId, preAuthorized, wired}], defaultNetworkId, allowPlayerSwitching, showSyncHint }` + `state.connection = { currentNetworkId, authenticated }`); **Layer 2** = content gating on that connection. Scene availability/dead zones deliberately don't apply to interfaces (a wired box works in a jam). Key mechanics:
- **Signal display is honest, wired is a flavor flag.** The mailbox-HUD bars light per the ACTIVE uplink's signal (`_uplinkContext` top-level `signal`/`sigLabel`; the network's configured `signalStrength`, app-wide thresholds 80/50/25) — they were four always-lit `<b>`s before ("CitiNet 75% but bars show 100"). Per-interface **`wired`** (config Network tab checkbox next to pre-authorized) = a physical LAN drop: signal reads 100, dropdown rows show `· wired`, the pill gets a small glyph, and **the HUD swaps the signal bars for a hardline icon entirely** (`.ncm-term__hud-wired`, `{{#if uplink.wired}}` branch). The wired glyph is **`fa-network-wired`, NOT `fa-ethernet`** ("looks like a comb" — dev). **Display/fiction only** — gating already ignores scene jamming for uplinks, so `wired` changes no mechanics.
- **`state.connection.currentNetworkId` uses the sentinel `'__DEFAULT__'`** (= follow `config.connection.defaultNetworkId`), NOT an absent key — whole-state rewrites (`_updateSession`, force-unlock) would otherwise convert "never set" into an explicit `null` and silently kill the boot default. `null` = explicitly disconnected (LOCAL ONLY). `TerminalService.getUplink` resolves sentinel → default → validates the network still exists AND is still an interface (else degrades to LOCAL ONLY). Logic sim: scratchpad `test-uplink.mjs` (12 cases).
- **Gating check is `networkVisibility`-shaped** (shard shape: `{restricted, allowedNetworks[], allowedTypes[]}`) via two service methods: `uplinkReaches(item, nv)` (raw, no GM bypass — used to badge **OFF-NET** chips for the GM) and `isContentVisible(item, nv)` (GM-bypassing enforcement). **Accounts** carry `account.network` — gated accounts **VANISH** for players (filtered in `getAccounts`, which also kills the secret-login path since `_resolveAuthAccount` goes through it); the GM keeps them with an OFF-NET chip. **Messages** carry per-message `networkVisibility` (persisted in `_buildMessageFlags` + read in `MessageRepository._pageToMessage` — the field-by-field gotcha, needed for composer edit-prefill); the mailbox filters them and shows a GM-toggleable **"N objects failed to sync"** hint (`showSyncHint`, counted within the current folder). **Exfil copies never carry `networkVisibility`** (a downloaded file is local — `createMessage` doesn't persist the key, `exfilToShard` doesn't pass it).
- **Network icons honor custom images**: the uplink dropdown rows and the network-gate cards render via `{{{ncm-net-icon theme}}}` (`_uplinkContext`/`_gateContext` expose the full `theme` block) — a network configured with `iconMode:'image'` + `customImage` in Network Manager shows its logo, like every other network list in the app. Wired interfaces keep the hardline-glyph override; `.ncm-net-icon-img` gets a fit rule (radius+padding) scoped to the terminal icon squares.
- **Switching**: the UPLINK pill (login hero — pre-login switching matters since gated accounts vanish — + mailbox HUD) opens a dropdown of interfaces (partial `templates/terminal/partials/uplink.hbs`, invoked `{{> … uplink align="right"}}` in the hero). GM writes via `TerminalService.setUplink`; **players relay** via `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_NET_REQUEST` (no RESULT op — the flag write syncs and the viewer re-renders off an `updateItem` hook registered in the constructor, removed in `close()`, skipped during boot/granted animations). A non-`preAuthorized` secured interface opens a **join-challenge console** (`ncm-term__netauth` overlay; the NETWORK's own security block; lockout keyed `${itemId}::net::${networkId}`; skill bypass defaults Interface/DV15 so core nets always give players a roll path; GM gets a "join without credentials" row); success stamps `state.connection.authenticated[networkId]` — the machine remembers.
- **Mid-session uplink switch away from a gated account drops the session** — `_prepareContext` detects the now-unreachable account and bounces to login ("UPLINK LOST").
- The composer's Network-Visibility fieldset is **no longer hidden for terminal targets** (the `{{#unless isTerminalTarget}}` wrap is gone; terminal targets get uplink-specific hint copy).

**Auth runs through the shared method-card dialog.** Selecting a secured account (`TerminalViewerApp._onSelectAccount`, card/roster/portrait styles) opens `NetworkAuthDialog.showForAccount(item, accountId)` — the SAME dialog networks use, with password + skill (`SkillService.performCheck`) + key-item + **strict AND/OR** (`authLogic`) + lockout (`SecurityService`, keyed `terminalId::accountId`) + Black ICE (`failureMode==='damage'`). The dialog was **parameterized via a source-adapter seam** (`scripts/ui/NetworkManagement/SecurityAuthAdapters.js`): `NetworkAuthDialog._adapter` abstracts the four source-specific ops — `refresh()` (fresh source-shaped `{id,name,type,theme,description,security}`), `checkPassword()`, `checkSkill()`, `grant()` — so `_prepareContext`/handlers stay source-agnostic and `this.networkId` is just the SecurityService key (a network id, or `terminalId::accountId`). `NetworkAuthAdapter` preserves network behavior byte-identically (delegates to `networkService.authenticate*`/`grantAuth`, which own their sounds + `_authenticatedNetworks` grant + events); `TerminalAuthAdapter` reads the account's SECURITY_BLOCK directly (password/DV compare, plays the sounds itself, `grant()` is a no-op — the dialog's resolve drives `_loginSuccess`). On the terminal side GM bypass is per-login (the dialog's GM-bypass button resolves success; the account's `gmBypassed` force-unlock still short-circuits `requiresAuth` before the dialog opens). **The lockscreen/keypad inline-password paths and the secret-login ("another account") console stay as quick-entry shortcuts** — single-method, no strict AND/OR (typed-username secret login has no known account until submit). **Player session-state writes RELAY (2026-07-13):** login/account-switch/logout write `terminalState.sessions` on the world item, which players can't update ("User Player lacks permission to update Item" was the first real player-login bug) — `TerminalService._updateSession` emits `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_SESSION_REQUEST` when `!isGM()` and the GM handler lands the write via `applySessionUpdate` (the uplink-relay pattern; no RESULT op — the flag write syncs back and the viewer re-renders off its updateItem hook; the viewer proceeds locally on its own `_accountId`, so the lag is invisible). The auth CHECK itself is client-side reads (the shard-relay trust model) — only the persistence relays.

**Login footprints + "N new since last login" (2026-08-14).** The machine records who logged into which account: `_updateSession`'s GM-side branch (the ONE chokepoint every session establishment lands in — same reason onLogin residents fire there) appends to **`terminalState.loginLog`** (`_appendLoginLog`: `{ts (game clock), accountId, username, operatorId, operatorName}`, capped `LOGIN_LOG_CAP` 50). Read via `getLoginLog(item)` / `getLastLogin(item, accountId)` (newest entry for one account). Consumers: (1) the ACCESS-GRANTED badge's **Last Login** field falls back authored `acct.lastLogin` → the previous footprint (`formatCyberDate`) → `FIRST LOGIN`; (2) an **"N new since last login"** line under the welcome (`_newSinceLastLogin` — counts non-sent/deleted/spam mail newer than the previous footprint; `.ncm-term__badge-newsince`); (3) Admin → Terminals detail shows **Recent Logins** (last 6, `recentLogins` in `TerminalsTab`, `.ncm-term-admin-footprint` rows). **Ordering matters:** `_loginSuccess` captures `this._prevLogin = getLastLogin(...)` **BEFORE** `recordLoginSuccess` writes — read-after-write would make the current login its own "previous" on the GM client while the relayed player client (whose write lags) saw the true previous; reading first makes both sides agree. **`relock` preserves `loginLog`** (it rewrites `terminalState` to defaults wholesale — the footprint log is the GM's audit trail and is explicitly carried over; note relock still wipes `residents`, pre-existing behavior). Sim: `tools/sims/test-footprints.mjs` (13/13).

**Exfil animations + burn picker.** Both exfil actions play an immersive full-viewer overlay before the notification resolves (`TerminalViewerApp._playExfilFx(kind, label)` → `.ncm-term__fx`, ~2.2s, reduced-motion-aware, awaited so the toast lands after): **download** = a server→Agent packet stream (cyan, `send`→`receive` sounds, "SAVED TO RECOVERED"); **burn** = a data-chip with a sweeping laser + embers (amber/red, `shard-compile`→`shard-insert` sounds, "BURN COMPLETE"). The **Burn-to-Shard target picker** (`_pickBurnTarget`) is the shared name picker (Carried / World chips, shards tagged first, `admin:false` so it wears the player theme) — existing shards sort first with a SHARD badge (burning onto one appends an entry; onto anything else converts it), GM world items are tagged WORLD, and CPR sheet-abstraction item types (`NON_CARRIER_TYPES`: skill/role/criticalInjury/netarch) are filtered out so a character's skill items don't drown the gear.

**Boot icon has three sources** (`boot.iconMode`: `preset` | `fa` | `image`): the theme preset's icon, a custom Font Awesome class (`boot.faIcon`), or a custom logo image (`boot.imageUrl`). `_bootContext` resolves them; the TerminalConfigApp Boot tab has a source dropdown that shows only the relevant field with a live preview chip (manual listeners — `_onRender` `syncBootIcon`; the FA-vs-image row visibility + preview update). `terminal-boot.hbs` renders `<img>` for image mode else `<i>`. (The arasaka-workstation preset icon was `fas fa-building-shield`, which isn't a valid FA6 free glyph → empty square; corrected to `fas fa-building`.) Image mode also has **`boot.imageFrame`**: `framed` (default — the themed icon square, `object-fit:cover`) vs **`free`** ("No frame" checkbox in the image row) — the logo renders at natural aspect with no box/border (a `drop-shadow` glow instead), via `--free` modifiers on `.ncm-term__boot-icon` / `.ncm-term__hero-logo` / the config preview (`_resolveBootIcon` exposes `imageFree`; wide corp logos looked crammed in the 88px square). The `--free img` rules must stay AFTER the framed img rules (equal specificity — order wins).

**Modular mailbox folders (2026-07-11) — LOCATION semantics.** `TerminalService.getFolders(item)` resolves the sidebar's folder set, ordered **Inbox → customs → Saved → Trash**: system folder **names** stay in `config.folderNames` (the preset contract), while **`config.folders` = `{ icons: {inbox,saved,trash}, savedEnabled, trashEnabled, custom: [{id,name,icon}] }`** layers icons, Saved/Trash visibility, and GM custom folders on top (legacy configs resolve to exactly the old three). **A message lives in exactly ONE location — Trash (`status.deleted`) > Saved (`status.saved`) > its custom folder (`folder` flag) > the Inbox** (the mailbox filters, the composer prefill, and the Move-to selector all resolve in that order; sim asserts every message matches exactly one filter). The `folder` flag persists in `_buildMessageFlags` + reads in `MessageRepository._pageToMessage` (the field-by-field gotcha); **`_buildMessageFlags` translates the system ids** (`folder:'saved'|'trash'` → the status flag, folder cleared) and **honors caller `status.saved/deleted`** (they were hardcoded false — the same gotcha that ate spam). **Unknown/removed folder ids fall back to the Inbox** at read time, so deleting a folder never hides mail. Filing surfaces: the composer Entry Header's **Folder select** lists ALL folders (terminal targets only; `_collectEntryData` translates system locs into `entryData.folder`+`entryData.status` because the EDIT path writes entryData keys straight to page flags, bypassing `_buildMessageFlags`), and the viewer detail header's GM **"Move to…" `<select>`** (`.ncm-term__move-select`, `moveOptions` in `_detailContext` — hidden Saved/Trash still offered as targets; manual `change` listener in `_onRender`, the select-vs-data-action gotcha; replaced the old bookmark/archive toggle chips — the red confirmed permanent-delete stays). `updateAccountMessage` accepts `folder`. Editor = the config **Identity tab** "Mailbox Folders" section: per-row icon `<select>` (`TERMINAL_FOLDER_ICONS`, curated FA6-free; an off-list saved value is injected at the top so re-saving can't silently change it) + name + "show" toggle (saved/trash) or remove button (customs); **Add folder** builds rows DOM-only (`_onFolderAdd`, `data-folder-row`/`data-f-*` attrs are the save contract — `_onSaveConfig` walks the rows into `folderNames` + `folders`). Sim: scratchpad `test-folders.mjs` (16/16 — real `getFolders` + `_buildMessageFlags` via `prototype.call`, one-location invariant). The full-preset library snapshots `folders` too.

**Exfil + the Recovered folder.** `allowExfil` (master switch) gates "To my Agent" (copy to the player's inbox) and "Burn to Shard" (`DataShardService.addMessage` onto a target item). Both **carry the original `contentType`/`contentData`** so a typed file (spreadsheet/syslog/banking/…) renders identically at the destination — `exfilToShard` once hardcoded `contentType: MESSAGE` and dropped the data, which degraded burns to plain mail. Copies are built via `TerminalService._exfilContentData`, which **neutralizes money** (EDDIES copies get `claimed:true`, BANKING copies `allowSiphon:false` — the `eddies: 0` no-double-grant rule; without it, siphoning the original AND the copy duplicated eddies). The provenance body header is prepended to plain messages only (typed bodies never render). Both also **preserve the original From + timestamp**; the inbox copy carries the original recipient as `to` and a `metadata.recovered/recoveredFrom/recoveredTo/recoveredOrigTs` block so it lands in a dedicated **"Recovered" folder** in the MessageViewer (excluded from Inbox/Unread; rendered with the cyan `.ncm-viewer__recovered-chip`), never masquerading as the downloader's own mail. **`MessageRepository.createMessage` builds metadata field-by-field**, so the `recovered*` keys had to be added there explicitly (the same gotcha that drops `spam`/unknown keys). Service exfil is GM-only (GM-owned journals); **players relay** via `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_EXFIL_REQUEST` → GM handler in `SocketHandlers.js` → `exfilTo*` → `TERMINAL_EXFIL_RESULT` notifies the requester (the shard-convert relay pattern).

**Theming** is per-terminal, scoped to `.ncm-terminal-viewer` via `data-terminal-preset` palette fallbacks in `terminal.css` (`TERMINAL_PRESETS`: arasaka-workstation/netwatch-unit/streetkid-bbs/retro-dos/ripperdoc — theme+security+boot+loginStyle+seed accounts). **`.ncm-terminal-viewer` carries a complete BASE `--ncm-*` palette** (brand defaults) so an unknown preset still renders self-contained — critical because `DEFAULTS.TERMINAL_CONFIG.theme.preset` is **`'classic'`, which has NO per-preset rule**; without the base palette every `--ncm-*` was undefined on a freshly-converted terminal, so the boot/login icon color resolved to inherited near-black on the dark bg = the "empty square" (an invisible glyph, not a missing one — headless can't reproduce it since FA doesn't load there). The per-preset rules are attribute selectors (higher specificity) so they still win for the five named presets. **Convert-to-Terminal re-renders the Items directory + open actor sheets immediately** (`ShardSheetOverride._refreshItemViews`, mirroring shard convert/remove) — previously the terminal badge/left-click-intercept only appeared after a manual refresh. The **boot-splash and login-hero icons share one resolver** (`_resolveBootIcon` → `{icon, imageUrl}`, honoring `boot.iconMode` preset|fa|image) so a configured custom icon/logo shows on both, never an empty box. **Custom (non-preset) theming is LIVE** — `ThemeService.applyTheme(prefs, targetEl)` is element-scoped (second param; document-global side effects like body classes/wallpaper only run when targeting `:root`, and `prefs.preset: null` skips the *player* THEME_PRESETS merge so a terminal can't inherit the Agent palette). The viewer applies `cfg.theme` on every render via `_applyCustomTheme` → the shared `buildTerminalThemePrefs(theme, presetKey)` (helpers.js): per key, saved override → `TERMINAL_PRESETS` palette → omitted (preset-attribute CSS / base palette shows through; a fresh terminal stores only `{preset:'classic'}` and renders unchanged). **All terminal.css `font-size`s go through `calc(Npx * var(--ncm-text-scale, 1))`** so the per-terminal Text Size slider works, isolated from the player's own scale. **The window-frame header follows the terminal theme too**: `_onRender` (and the config's `_applyThemeLive` preview loop) apply the same prefs to the **app root element** (`this.element`), because the `.window-header` (title spans `.ncm-term__title-*`, header controls) sits OUTSIDE `.ncm-terminal-viewer` and its `--ncm-*` lookups otherwise resolve from `:root` — the PLAYER's theme. `terminal.css` styles `.ncm-terminal-app .window-header` (bg-deep + primary-tinted border, control icons forced on both the button AND its inner `<i>` — the admin-theme window-chrome precedent) with `!important` against Foundry's frame styles. Don't move the viewer's inline vars UP to only the app root: the per-preset `[data-terminal-preset]` attribute rules on the viewer element would then out-specific the inherited customs. The config **Theme tab mirrors the player ThemeCustomizer** (it literally reuses the `.ncm-tc__` widget classes — theme-customizer.css's `.ncm-app`-scoped layout rules cross-apply; its `.ncm-tc`-root-scoped button/select resets are co-scoped to `.ncm-term-config`, keep the two selector lists in sync): a live-preview strip + font-preview box (class `.ncm-ttc-live` — `_applyThemeLive` applies the draft onto them AND any open viewer of this item), preset swatch-cards (incl. a "Classic" card = no-preset brand look; a card click stages palette+fonts after stripping prior overrides), a 9-key color-override grid with override dots + reset, font selects, and Text Size/Contrast sliders. **The theme save contract is `_themeDraft`, NOT form inputs** — theme controls are unnamed/data-keyed, mutate the draft via manual listeners (no re-render, so edits survive tab switches — the pre-rebuild bug was that any tab switch silently discarded them), `_onSave` persists the draft (`theme: deepClone(draft ?? cfg.theme)`) and clears it; closing unsaved reverts previewed viewers by re-rendering them. **Saved Themes** live in the world setting `terminalThemePresets` (`[{name, theme}]`, registered in SettingsManager) — save/apply/overwrite/delete rows + theme-only JSON import/export (`{ncmTerminalTheme:1, theme}` envelope, bare object accepted). The old "Apply preset" button is gone — a preset card click stages the full kit (see the login-styles section's `_presetKit` notes). **Boot** reuses the shard-style choreography; boot log lines auto-reveal via staggered CSS (`ncm-term-bootline`) so the splash is never empty. The **ACCESS GRANTED** state is the "credential keycard" design (`_grantedContext` → `ncm-term__badge*`), redesigned 2026-08-14 to a slicker **3-beat sequence** (~2.3s): (1) the keycard **materialises** from a top scanline sliver (`ncm-term-badgemat`, a `clip-path` reveal with **NO `forwards`** so it reverts to `none` at rest — otherwise a persisted `inset(0)` clips the card's box-shadow glow) while corner brackets draw in (`ncm-term__badge-brk`); (2) a single **reader line** (`ncm-term__badge-reader`) sweeps down the whole card once — each field's VALUE **types in** left-to-right (`clip-path`, staggered by inline `--r`, blinking cursor via `.ncm-term__badge-val::after`) and a **hairline** along the bottom edge (`ncm-term__badge-prog-fill`) fills primary→green in step; (3) the card settles to a **green validated edge-glow** (`ncm-term-badgevalid` pulses then holds) as a green **check badge pops onto the portrait's lower-right corner** (`ncm-term__badge-chk` — the photo is `overflow:visible` and the `<img>` self-clips via its own `border-radius`, so the badge lays ON TOP and isn't clipped), then **ACCESS GRANTED** rises (`login-success` sound; **2.7s** hold before mailbox — timings are hardcoded in the CSS delays, keep them in sync with the hold in `_onRender`). The reader + hairline + brackets all ride **`--ncm-primary`** (the machine's identity colour, never `--ncm-secondary`) so on a red-primary preset nothing sweeps foreign blue. Theme-aware (accents = `--ncm-primary`, verify = `--ncm-success`); mock `docs/mockups/terminal-login-animation-mock.html`, port preview `terminal-login-port-preview.html`. (This replaced a **busier** version — per-row checkmarks + an AUTHENTICATING→VERIFIED progress bar + a full-card scan light-bar + a VALIDATED chip all at once over ~3s — the dev found it too busy; don't reintroduce those, and don't bring back the earlier diagonal green rubber-stamp / RGB-glitch either.) The badge is driven by **per-account credential fields** (`TERMINAL_ACCOUNT`: `role`, `clearance`, `idNumber`, `classification`, `location`, `lastLogin`), all editable in the config's **Accounts → Credential Badge** section, each **falling back** to terminal metadata (classification/location) or a derived default (clearance ← `isAdmin`, idLabel ← email/username, lastLogin ← "FIRST LOGIN"). So classification (RESTRICTED/SECRET/TOP SECRET…) can differ per account. Wired through `_grantedContext` → `badge` + `_editorContext`/`_onSaveAccount` (`acct*` fields). `role` is now editable (it was previously only seedable via presets). **`_bootContext` must return the boot fields directly** (`{ icon, title, … }`), NOT wrapped in `{ boot: … }` — it's assigned `ctx.boot = this._bootContext(...)`, so a wrapper double-nests to `ctx.boot.boot` and the template's `{{boot.icon}}`/`{{boot.title}}` render undefined = **a blank glowing icon square + no title/subtitle on the boot screen** (the loading bar still shows since it has no data binding). This bug hid behind hand-written headless tests that hardcoded the icon class instead of going through the context — the lesson: verify data-bound values through the real `_prepareContext`, not just static markup. (The other state contexts use `Object.assign(ctx, this._xContext())` and so return `{ … }` maps; boot is the odd one assigned to a named key.)

**Login styles + machine personality (2026-07-10 batch).** `config.loginStyle` (one of `TERMINAL_LOGIN_STYLES`: `cards` default · `crt` · `roster` · `lockscreen` · `keypad` · `portraits`) drives the login screen's LOOK — same accounts/security/auth flow underneath, all styles read `--ncm-*` so the terminal theme applies. `terminal-login.hbs` branches on style flags from `_loginContext` (root carries `data-login-style`). **cards/roster/portraits** route secured picks to the SECURE SHELL console as before; **crt** is a full-screen phosphor tty whose account lines also route there (the console is already console-styled, so the fiction holds); **lockscreen/keypad** feature ONE account (`_focusAccountId`, default = `defaultAccountId`; mini-avatar row refocuses via `terminalFocusAccount`, open accounts log straight in) with **inline password entry** — `terminalInlineAuth` binds the account from `closest('[data-account-id]')` (the keypad wrapper carries it) and runs the normal `_onManualAuth` (same lockout/ICE; failures render `inlineError` on the login screen). Keypad digits are wired in `_onRender` (pad buttons write a visually-hidden `manualPassword` input + echo dots on the LCD; ⏎ submits; non-password accounts get an "other access methods" link to the console). More personality knobs: `config.login = { motd, welcomeText }` (MOTD banner under the hero / custom ACCESS GRANTED line via `_grantedContext.welcomeText`); `config.folderNames = { inbox, saved, trash }` (diegetic mailbox labels, blank = defaults — CASE FILES / EVIDENCE / PURGED; see the **modular folders** paragraph below — `config.folders` layers icons/visibility/customs on top, names deliberately stay here for preset compatibility); `config.screensaver = { enabled, idleSeconds, text }` (idle dim + drifting marquee — `_resetScreensaver` re-arms on every render and on any pointer/key inside the window via a constructor-registered document-capture listener removed in `close()`; real-time `setTimeout` is deliberate); `theme.wallpaper` + `theme.wallpaperStrength` (**two intensity tiers**, both scaled by the 5–100 Theme-tab slider writing `--ncm-wallpaper-strength` [stored 0–100, applied ÷100, default 25]: **full-screen states — login / secure-shell `.ncm-term__mwrap` / ACCESS-GRANTED `.ncm-term__granted` / network gate — render it as the base background** at `min(1, strength × 2.6)` ≈ 65% by default, per dev feedback "it should just BE the login background", while the **mailbox panes stay subtle** at strength × 0.8 ≈ 20%; the config Theme-tab preview strip `.ncm-tc__preview.ncm-ttc-live` gets a wallpaper `::before` too so the slider visibly works without an open viewer. **The ACTUAL "wallpaper doesn't work" root cause: a relative `url()` inside a CSS custom property resolves against the stylesheet that USES the var** — terminal.css → `/modules/…/styles/components/worlds/…` → silent 404 — NOT the document base; `applyTheme` now roots the path via `foundry.utils.getRoute` (typeof-guarded — the ImagePopout lexical-global lesson; honors a route prefix; bare `/` fallback outside Foundry). It always clears both vars when unset and escapes `'` in the url. **Layering: each wallpaper host gets `isolation: isolate` + a `z-index:-1` ::before** (paints above the host's own background, below ALL content — no `> *` position/z-index overrides), and host `position: relative` goes through **`:where()`** so it can never out-order an existing scheme — a flat `position:relative` list once overrode `.ncm-term__granted`'s `position:absolute; inset:0` and collapsed the ACCESS GRANTED card to the top of the flow. Verified end-to-end through the REAL ThemeService/buildTerminalThemePrefs in headless Chrome, 8/8 (`test-wallpaper2.html` / `wallpaper2.png` — URL resolution, tier opacities, centering intact)) and `theme.scanlines/flicker/glitch` (per-terminal effects — `data-term-scanlines/flicker` attrs set in `_onRender` + glitch riding `--ncm-glitch-intensity`, 0–100 in config ÷100 in `buildTerminalThemePrefs`). **Per-terminal sound sets are LIVE** — `config.soundSet` names a `TERMINAL_SOUND_SETS` entry (constants.js: `default` · `corp-chime` · `street-bbs` · `med-soft` · `mil-hardline` · `ghost-wall`); a set **remaps the terminal's sound ids onto other existing `SOUND_PATHS` assets with playback-rate + volume shaping** (no new audio files — the `playClick` Web Audio precedent, via the new public `SoundService.playStyled(id, {volume, rate})`, which short-circuits to `play()` at rate 1 and falls back un-pitched if Web Audio fails). Resolution is `resolveTerminalSound(setKey, soundId)` (helpers.js — unknown set/unmapped id = identity, so legacy configs are untouched). **Every terminal-played sound must route through it**: `TerminalViewerApp._playTermSound` (overlay-aware — a staged preset kit previews its sound set too) covers boot/login/net-join/exfil-FX, and `TerminalAuthAdapter._play` covers the method-card dialog's password/skill results. `TerminalService`'s convert/relock sounds are deliberately unrouted (GM authoring feedback, not machine personality). Picker = the Boot & Login tab's **Sound Profile** card row (hidden `soundSet` input, the login-style-card save contract) with an **Audition** button that plays boot → login-success → login-fail through the selected set (real `setTimeout` — UI feedback, not game time). Each `TERMINAL_PRESETS` entry carries a `soundSet`, staged/saved with the kit like loginStyle. (Related fix: `SOUND_PATHS['shard-decrypt']` pointed at the nonexistent `shard/decrypt.ogg` while the real file is `security/shard-decrypt.ogg` — the shard-unlock splash AND the terminal login flourish were silently mute; the sound-set logic sim in scratchpad `test-soundsets.mjs` now asserts every referenced asset exists on disk.) The screensaver also carries `iconMode` (`boot` follow-the-boot-icon | `fa` | `image` | `none`), `faIcon`/`imageUrl`, and `motion` (`drift`/`bounce`/`static` — `data-ss-motion` on the overlay), resolved by `_screensaverContext`. The Boot & Login tab has **live previews for both the login style and the screensaver**: `_syncLoginPreview` builds a mini mock of the selected style from the terminal's REAL accounts (`.ncm-cfg-lprev-*`), `_syncSsPreview` mirrors the logo/text/motion (`.ncm-cfg-ssprev`). **TERMINAL_PRESETS is now a 12-entry library** (original 5 + trauma-team / militech-checkpoint / ncpd-precinct / nomad-rig / bd-lounge / maelstrom-chopshop / blackwall-relay), each bundling theme+security+boot+loginStyle+**login (MOTD/welcome)+screensaver+folderNames**+seed accounts — every built-in is a COMPLETE terminal kit. **A preset card stages the FULL KIT** (the "not obvious presets affect boot" fix): palette+fonts ride `_themeDraft` (live preview), and `_presetKit` (the preset KEY) overlays the Boot & Login/Identity tabs' context (boot fields, login style, MOTD/welcome, screensaver, folder names) — so Save reads everything straight from the (kit-prefilled) DOM inputs, then seeds the preset's accounts only when the terminal has none; a staged kit shows an amber notice bar on BOTH the Theme and Boot & Login tabs, with a **"palette only"** escape (`clearPresetKit`). Kit cleared on save/close. **Open viewers preview the staged state live** via the config-preview channel: `_applyThemeLive` pushes an overlay (`{theme, boot, loginStyle, login, screensaver, folderNames}`) to every open viewer of the item → `TerminalViewerApp.setConfigPreview(overlay, {rerender})`; the viewer's `_effectiveConfig()` **shallow-merges** it over the saved config (shallow on purpose — the draft theme's deleted overrides must fall through, a deep merge would resurrect them) and all render paths read it. `rerender` fires only on KIT transitions — palette-only updates ride cheap inline vars so slider drags can't cause render storms. Overlay cleared (rerender:true) on save/close/"palette only". **Boot entrance animations are real now** — `BOOT_ANIMATIONS` (standard-fade / holographic-snap / scan-sweep / warm-dissolve / glitch-flood / crt-power-on), CSS variants keyed by `data-boot-animation` on BOTH `.ncm-term__boot` and the config preview splash (picking an animation chip replays it in the preview via attr-swap + forced reflow, and the preview strip has an explicit **▶ REPLAY** button — `[data-boot-replay]`); legacy/unknown values render the base look. **GM EDIT gear on the login screen** (`.ncm-term__login-gm`, hero stat + CRT top bar, gated `isGM`) opens the config without logging in. **Full Terminal Preset Library** (Theme tab, world setting `terminalPresetLibrary`): "Save This Terminal" snapshots theme/boot/loginStyle/login/screensaver/folderNames/metadata/soundSet + account SEEDS (`_collectFullPreset` strips ids + masterContactId/actorId — world links don't travel); Apply confirms, overwrites those config blocks, and seeds accounts only when the terminal has none; rows offer overwrite/export/delete and the JSON envelope is `{ncmTerminalPreset:1, name, config}` (import appends to the library, never auto-applies). The config **Boot & Login tab** (renamed from Boot) mirrors the Theme tab's live-preview discipline: a data-bound boot-splash preview strip, an icon-source segmented control with a **searchable visual icon picker** (`TERMINAL_ICON_LIBRARY` — curated FA6-FREE classes only; an invalid class renders an EMPTY square, the arasaka lesson) + free-class input + image FilePicker, animation/speed **chips**, a "generate flavor lines" dice (`BOOT_FLAVOR_LINES`), **login-style cards**, and MOTD/welcome/screensaver fields — all mirrored into hidden inputs bearing the ORIGINAL field names (the account-editor save contract; wiring in `_wireBootTab`). Wallpaper/effects live in the **Theme tab** as draft keys (live-preview + Save like colors; effects preview only on an open viewer, the strip can't show them).

**Authoring surfaces.** GM: right-click an item → **Convert to Terminal** (instant + opens `TerminalConfigApp`) / Open / Configure / Remove (`ShardSheetOverride`, directory badge + left-click-to-open mirror the shard pattern; unconvert re-renders the directory so the badge clears). The `TerminalConfigApp` (tabs Identity/Accounts/Theme/Boot/Network/Exfil, reuses `ncm-cfg-*` styling, import/export JSON) and an in-viewer GM **EDIT** gear + per-message edit/delete + New Message + **Saved/Trash toggles** (bookmark = `status.saved`, archive-box↔restore = `status.deleted`, written per-leaf via `TerminalService.updateAccountMessage({status})`; the red trash-can stays the separate confirmed *permanent* delete). Admin Panel → **Terminals tab** (`TerminalsTab`, tab key `6`) is a **split-panel manager** (2026-07-11 redesign, NetworkManagement-Scenes pattern): summary strip → toolbar (debounced search over name/org/preset/account names, sort `<select>` [manual `change` listener — the standing gotcha], All/Online/Locked filter chips, Import) → left = paginated row list (10/page, `TerminalsTab.PAGE_SIZE`, selection persists via `_selectedId`, auto-selects first visible) → right = the selected terminal's detail (identity+classification, facts strip: preset/login style/sound set/uplink [`getUplink().network?.name` else LOCAL ONLY]/reach/exfil, account chips with lock/admin/hidden/net-gated badges, live sessions, actions). **"Reset" = `TerminalService.relock`** (it already wipes sessions + lockouts + gmBypass and returns the uplink to default — the button is just honestly labeled now); **Export** downloads the full config as `{ncmTerminal:1, name, config}` JSON. New actions `terminalSelectAdmin`/`terminalFilterAdmin`/`terminalPageAdmin`/`terminalExportAdmin` follow the static-delegator pattern. **Buttons are the tab's own `.ncm-term-admin-btn`** (text buttons: auto width, mono uppercase, `--primary`/`--danger`/`--icon` variants; Remove sits right of a flex spacer) — do NOT use the shared `.ncm-action-btn` here, it's a **26px icon button** (tab-shards.css) and text labels inside it render crushed against the borders. The detail facts are a **labeled key/value grid** (`.ncm-term-admin-fact`, auto-fill minmax(128px,1fr): Uplink · Reach · Exfil · Preset · Login · Sound · Location), not icon chips. Layout headless-verified against the real admin theme (`term-admin2.png`).

**Config sheet scroll contract:** `terminal-config.hbs`'s root MUST carry `.ncm-cfg` and the panel wrapper MUST be `.ncm-cfg-content` — that pair (flex column + `flex:1; overflow-y:auto`, from shard-config.css) is the ONLY scroll path, because `.ncm-item-inbox-config .window-content` is `overflow:hidden !important`. A rename to anything else (the original build used a CSS-less `.ncm-cfg-panels`) makes every tab unscrollable and Save unreachable.

**Account editor UX (mock-approved rebuild, config-account.hbs + `_wireAccountEditor`).** Six widgets, all wired via manual listeners in `_onRender`: **(A) Link Identity picker** — live search over master contacts + world actors (`_identityCandidates()`, actors deduped when already linked to a contact); picking autofills name/email/portrait **DOM-only** (`_applyIdentityPick` — a render would discard unsaved edits) and stages the link in `this._editorLink`, which overrides the stored link in `_editorContext` and at save (reset on add/edit/cancel/save). **(B)** portrait tile = the FilePicker button. **(C)** clearance chips (`CLEARANCE_PRESETS` + AUTO + custom) and classification pills. **(D)** last-access chips stamping the game clock (`timeService.getCurrentTimeMs` → `formatCyberDate`) + datetime-local fallback; free text stays legal (badge flavor, not a timer). **(E)** security method cards with toggle knobs (hidden checkbox + styled knob), skill `<select>` from `SKILL_MAP` (a legacy value is kept in the list), key-item picker (the shared name picker), password generator (`PASS_WORDS`). **(F)** sticky Save footer (`.ncm-acct-foot`, sticks to the `.ncm-cfg-content` scrollport) + dirty indicator. **The save contract is unchanged:** chips/pills mirror their state into hidden inputs bearing the ORIGINAL field names (`acctClearance`/`acctClassification`/`secAuthLogic`/`secFailureMode`), so `_onSaveAccount` reads one contract — keep it that way when adding widgets.

**Accounts ↔ GM Master Contacts (link-or-create).** On account save, a non-empty email resolves against the master directory: `getByEmail` match → link; no match → `MasterContactService.addContact` (name/email/portrait/role/org/location + a terminal-provenance note) — so terminal identities are real, portrait-bearing directory entries. Precedence: an explicit picker choice/unlink this session (`_editorLink`) → the stored link; an existing link survives unless the email now matches a *different* contact; the account pulls the linked contact's portrait when it has none. Unlink + save re-links if the email still matches (documented in the editor hint).

**Scene access pins (drag a terminal onto the canvas).** `scripts/integrations/TerminalScenePins.js` (registered in `registerTerminalSystem`, ready/64, instance at `game.nightcity.terminalScenePins`). A GM drags a terminal item onto a scene (or right-clicks it → **Place on Current Scene**) → a **map Note** linked to the terminal's journal is created at the drop point (icon = the item's art, `iconSize` 64, label = `terminalName`/item name, flags `terminalItemId`+`terminalItemUuid`). Players **double-click** the pin to log in: the core `activateNote` hook returns `false` (suppressing the journal sheet) and calls `game.nightcity.openTerminal`. Design constraints that must not regress:
- **Drop interception is a capture-phase `document` `drop` listener, NOT just a `dropCanvasData` hook.** Core binds the canvas drop as an `ondrop` **property** on `#board`, and every drop-handling module (Item Piles turns dropped items into loot piles) rides the `dropCanvasData` hook fired inside it — so a document-capture listener wins **deterministically regardless of module load order**. It claims the event ONLY for `isTerminal` items (`preventDefault` + `stopImmediatePropagation`); everything else — including data shards, which SHOULD become Item Piles loot — passes through untouched. A `dropCanvasData` hook returning `false` stays as belt-and-braces. Players dropping a terminal get a "GM only" warn.
- **Pins are only visible/clickable when the core client toggle `notesDisplayToggle` is on — and it defaults OFF.** World setting `terminalPinsAutoShow` (default on, in Module Settings) flips it at ready on every client. Note visibility also requires OBSERVER on the journal — terminal journals are created OBSERVER, so don't tighten that ownership.
- **Optional proximity gate:** `config.scenePin.range` (grid squares, 0 = off; field in the config **Network tab → Scene Access Point**) — a non-GM needs an owned, non-hidden token within range (+0.5-square grace so "adjacent" counts) or the click is denied with a warn + `error` sound. Distance is plain center-to-center pixels ÷ grid size. Opening from the item itself is deliberately ungated.
- **Cleanup:** `deleteItem` and the unconvert flag-wipe (`-=MODULE_ID` or `-=isTerminal` in `updateItem` changes) remove that terminal's pins across all scenes, gated to `game.users.activeGM` so multi-GM setups delete once. Stale pins (item gone) warn on click instead of opening. Multiple pins of one terminal are allowed.
- **Known compat caveat:** `Hooks.call('activateNote')` stops at the first `false`, so a journal-takeover module registered earlier (e.g. Monk's Enhanced Journal) could swallow pin clicks. No clean fix; revisit if a user reports it.
- Logic-simmed via the real class + mocked globals (scratchpad `test-scenepins.mjs`, 31/31 — drop claiming both paths, coord transform, range gate, cleanup).

**Test setup:** `tools/test-terminal-setup.macro.js` — paste into a Script macro to build a fully-authored test terminal (4 accounts incl. hidden, priority-coded messages) and open it. **`tools/test-upload-setup.macro.js`** is its UPLOAD/PLANT counterpart: it seeds the design's on-ramp — a go-bag shard ("Ghost's Go-Bag") of four **PLANT** forgeries at escalating forge DVs (chatlog/gallery/dossier/document) to carry to a terminal and push via OPERATOR TOOLS → UPLOAD. Two things it encodes that bite otherwise: the shard must be **actor-owned** (`_gatherCarriedPrograms` scans `actor.items` only — a world item is invisible to the picker, and the actor must match the HUD's OPERATOR chip), and it seeds **no EXECUTE** entry on purpose (`_gatherCarriedPrograms` sets `disabled: family === 'execute'` until thread #6, and `runUpload` only installs `plant` residents — a payload/malware entry would render as a dead SOON row).

**Planned work (backlog — not built yet; full detail in the `terminal-expansion` memory note's ▶ RESUME HERE):**
1. Rest of the per-type locked/encrypted states (roll-to-unlock slice 1 SHIPPED 2026-07-11: gallery frames / sealed avlog / document-dossier-spreadsheet DECLASSIFY + traceOnFail; schematic markers #33; calendar events #34; dossier masked-identity + encrypted combat profile #35 — remaining candidates: record rows, chatlog, per-entry variants).
2. ~~PAYLOAD type overhaul~~ — SHIPPED 2026-07-12 (utility-program identity, module-native effects, timed active/kill, disguise trojan, hybrid manifest→console→active card; see the `payload` type bullet above). Follow-up candidates: a player-facing "analyze any file" SNIFF affordance (today disguise reveal is GM-driven), effect appliers for more host types, sound-cue pass on execute.
3. Config-sheet polish pass (dev feedback pending) · sound-set rate/volume ear-tuning · thread-transcript typed-body cosmetic gap · spreadsheet column sort · player-relay uplink two-client verify. (Relayed-write lag FIXED 2026-07-12 — the viewer's journal-page hooks.)
   (Everything else from earlier backlog lists SHIPPED — §8c catalog 18/18, method-card auth dialog, sound sets, SecurityConsole + 6-action exfil, image lightbox, entry templates/examples, From↔Master-List both directions, sender add/reply chips, Admin Contacts per-role grouping + group-by selector, Admin Terminals split-panel manager.)
4. **UPLOAD / FORGE — the missing player→machine direction (NEXT BIG ARC, design first).** Every content action today flows terminal→player (siphon/exfil/claim/save-frame). Upload is the inverse — the netrunner "get in, leave something behind." Mock: `docs/mockups/eddies-and-upload-mock.html`. The insight: it's mostly **composition of what exists** (composer authors 18 types · payloads carry module-native effects · shards carry typed entries as a go-bag · SecurityConsole runs skill-gated actions w/ trace-on-fail). Two families = dev's two examples: **PLANT** (upload forged content — chatlog/gallery/dossier — that the next login finds; the blackmail case; +a FORGE check, botched forgery leaves tells) and **EXECUTE** (payload effect engine pointed at a host you don't own — wipe a log, jam uplink, forge a backdoor account, leave Black ICE). Dev's on-ramp ask: an **NPC hands the player a shard** (or a message) → carry it to a terminal → **upload through the Agent or the shard** → security gates the push. Recommended phasing: **P1 PLANT** (cheapest, delivers blackmail now) → **P2 EXECUTE** (malware gets authored effects + becomes uploadable; malware/payload converge) → **P3 a real creator UI**. **P1 hard requirement:** a GM audit view for "what's been planted where" (forged content must be trackable). Also **discuss the malware GAME MODEL holistically** before building — dev flagged it's worth its own conversation.
5. **TRACE needs teeth + a persistent GM log (dev: "if a player does something the trace needs more consequence… at least the GM needs to see it and have a log of it").** Current state is inconsistent and EPHEMERAL: `_fireUnlockTrace` (unlock/camera/malware-sniff paths) plays trace-fire + `showShardTrace` splash **and** whispers the GM a client-created ChatMessage — but **siphon and malware-deploy call `showShardTrace` bare** (no whisper, no record), and **nothing persists anywhere**. Design a real trace subsystem: (a) ONE `fireTrace({actor, source, action, dv?, host?})` every trace path routes through (kill the bare `showShardTrace` calls); (b) a **durable log** (world setting/flag — who traced what, where, when) surfaced in the Admin Panel (Messages or a new Security tab); (c) escalating **consequences** to discuss — GM alert (always), a per-terminal/network "heat" counter, lockout on repeat traces, an ICE response, a countdown timer (the `traceStarted/traceExpiresAt` fields already exist on messages but aren't wired to anything). The trace timer P3 hook in `DEFAULTS.TERMINAL_CONFIG` was scaffolded and never built — fold it in.

## Architecture: Upload / Forge / Trace / Operator Gate / Security tab (2026-07-14→15)

The player→machine arc (backlog #4/#5 above, now BUILT — design doc `docs/UPLOAD-FORGE-TRACE.md` is the source of truth). All new, all UNCOMMITTED, verified via scratchpad sims + faithful headless renders. **Not yet live-tested in Foundry** — the dev breaks here.

**Trace subsystem — `scripts/services/TraceService.js`.** ONE funnel, two entry points: **`fireTrace(o)`** (a FAILURE — plays trace-fire + `showShardTrace`, bumps heat, GM-alerts, logs) and **`logSuccess(o)`** (a SUCCESS — logs with `success:true`, **zero heat**, so the Activity Log is a *full* feed the GM can audit, "no heat gained" shown explicitly). Both build a `_payload(o)` carrying `actor/operator`, `action`, `severity`, `targetKind` (terminal/network/shard/message/contact/account), `targetUuid`/`targetMessageId`/`targetContactId` (deep-link anchors), `outcome`, `roll`. `recordTrace` appends to the `traceLog` world setting (cap `LOG_CAP=250`) and applies `heatDelta` (`HEAT_BY_SEVERITY` minor:10/major:20/hostile:35; success=0). Heat lives in the `traceHeat` world setting keyed by `operator::objectId`; `getHeat/bumpHeat/setHeat`, decay `HEAT_DECAY_PER_HR=5` (poll-based, through the time provider), tiers `TIERS` 25/50/80 (`tierOf` static → cold/warm/hot/burned). `_heatChat` posts a GM-reason card on manual adjust; `_gmAlert` respects the `securityAlertLevel` client setting. Countdowns: `startCountdown/cancelCountdown/reapCountdowns` (the "corp found you" escalation timer). **GM-owned world writes → players relay via `SOCKET_OPS.TRACE_REQUEST`.** Every prior bare `showShardTrace` call site (31 of them) was routed through `fireTrace`.

**Upload / PLANT — `scripts/ui/content/UploadFlow.js` + `UploadConsole.js` (+ `styles/components/upload-console.css`).** The inverse of exfil: carry a forged typed-entry (from a shard "go-bag" or composed) to a terminal and leave it behind. `runUpload` opens the FUI picker (OperatorSelect pattern — framework-free promise modal appended into `host`), runs a **FORGE check**, and installs the content as a resident (below). `forgeQualityFromMargin(margin)` → a 0–100% quality; `stealthDVFromQuality` → the scan DV a later ANALYZE must beat (a botched forgery = low quality = easy to detect = "leaves tells").

**THE FORGE CHECK IS A LADDER, NOT PASS/FAIL** (`forgeOutcome(total, dv)`, `FORGE_SLOPPY_BAND = 4`): `margin >= 0` → **clean** · `-1..-4` → **sloppy** (it lands, but ANALYZE will flag it FORGED) · **worse than -4 → rejected, NOTHING installs**. The original model installed on any committed roll with no floor, so failing by 12 planted the same file as failing by 1. `runSecurityConsole` gained an optional **`verdictFor({ok,total,dv}) → {label, tone}`** hook for exactly this (a binary ok/fail can't express three rungs) plus an **`is-warn`** amber stage tone (a sloppy forge is a compromised success, so no shake). The console's intro spells the ladder out before the player commits. **Don't bother hiding the DV** — the shared `templates/chat/hack-result.hbs` prints "vs DV {dc}" for every check in the module, so hiding it in one console is cosmetic-only; it would need a `hideDV` flag threaded through `SkillService.performCheck` + that card (which every hack shares).

**Re-uploading REPLACES, it does not stack (`residentSlotKey` + `installResident`).** A resident carries a `slotKey` (`effectType::accountId::name`); installing one **replaces an existing ARMED resident in the same slot**, and only an armed one (a *fired* plant already landed, so a re-upload is genuinely a new file; slotless/legacy residents never dedupe). Without this, a player who missed the forge and retried left N armed duplicates and the GM's next login prompt fired **every one of them** — the reported "it uploads all the failed tries as well". Sim: scratchpad `test-forge-bands.mjs` (21/21, drives the real `installResident` via `prototype.call`). The COMPILING beat streams procedural code via **`HackFX.streamHackCode(el,{durationMs,subject,family,reduced,onDone})`** — family-aware pools (plant vs execute, with warning lines), reduced-motion-gated. **Composer** gained an uploadable fieldset (author the payload up front).

**Residents (triggered effects) — in `TerminalService`.** A planted file can be a **resident**: `getResidents/installResident/updateResident/removeResident`, lifecycle `_checkLoginResidents` (fires on next login) / `_proposeResidentFire` / `fireResident` / `reapResidents` / `checkActionResidents`. Stored in `TERMINAL_STATE.residents`. `applyMalwareEffect` is the shared effect applier. This is the "fires on next login" armed-plant mechanic surfaced in the Security tab's Planted & Resident card. **Only `plant` is implemented** in `fireResident`; `keylog`/`forgeAccount`/`logicBomb`/`deployICE` fall through to "GM to adjudicate" until thread #6.

**A trigger match PROPOSES — and the world setting `residentFireMode` decides.** `propose` (default) → the GM gets the prompt · `auto` → fires on trigger, GM is just told · `manual` → never prompts, waits for Admin → Security's Fire. **The pause exists because a plant firing may be the moment the table runs the NET** (design §5) — but that intent was completely invisible, so the prompt now says it in as many words. The prompt is **`scripts/ui/content/ResidentConsole.js` + `styles/components/resident-console.css`** (`runResidentConsole` → `'fire'|'defuse'|'later'`): the FUI family modal (framework-free promise, appended into `TerminalService._openViewerElement(item)` so it inherits *that machine's* preset; body-fallback), replacing a raw `Dialog` with inline styles. It shows what fired, who planted it, the target mailbox, the **forgery tell** (clean/passable/sloppy from `forgeQuality`), names the **closest NET action** for the effect (`NET_VERB`), and points at the setting for tables that don't play it that way. ESC/backdrop = "later" (never destructive). **Its markup is a template literal — keep backticks out of it** (one inside an HTML comment terminated the string and the modal silently never rendered). Preview: `docs/mockups/resident-console-preview.html` (imports the REAL module against the real cascade).

**Operator capability gate — `canOperate(actor, override)` (helpers.js) + `DEFAULTS.OPERATOR_GATE` (constants.js).** Restricts WHO can operate a terminal/run content actions: `mode` (everyone / only-who-qualifies), `roleLogic`, `roles[]`, `skill`+min-rank, **`itemGroups`** (grouped **OR-of-ANDs** — each group is an AND of required items, groups OR together; items are `{name,img}` so the gate UI shows item images), `actors[]`, `allowGM`. `nameOf` handles both string and `{name,img}`. Gate config is the `operatorGate` world setting, edited in the Security tab.

**Admin → Security tab (tab key `7`) — `scripts/ui/AdminPanel/tabs/SecurityTab.js` + `templates/admin-panel/tab-security.hbs` + `styles/components/admin-panel/tab-security.css`.** Composed BaseTab (static delegators on `AdminPanelApp`, ~20 actions `security*`). Five cards: **summary strip** (events 24h / running hot / planted / recycle bin), **Activity Log** (the trace feed — filter chips All/Serious/Fumbles, alert-level select, search, pagination, "N new since last check" via the `securityLogSeen` client setting; color-coded target icons per `targetKind`; each row's ↗ **deep-links to the exact object** — terminal/shard/network via `game.nightcity` launchers, message via journal-page scan, contact via `GMContactManagerApp`), **Heat** (per operator·object bars with tier legend, GM **Adjust** [reason→chat], **Clear**/**Clear all**, **Escalate** [countdown + ICE `Dialog` → `ICEService.applyDamage`] gated on value≥50, **Defuse**), **Planted & Resident** (armed plants, Fire now / Defuse / Remove), **Recycle Bin** (GM Restore / Purge / Empty bin for wiped files), **Operator Access** (the gate editor — skill dropdown from `SKILL_MAP`, role chips, grouped gear chip-groups with item images, `+ add an OR option`). **The gear/role choosers are the shared name picker (`showNamePicker` from `onGateAddItem`/`onGateAddRole`), not `<select>`s** — the burn-picker/key-item precedent (search box + item art + click-to-pick; already-chipped rows render `.is-taken`, greyed and click-refused). A native dropdown had no search and no art, and it forced `_gateContext` to scan **every actor's items on every render** to fill the options; the pickers now read `_availableItems()`/`_availableRoles()` **on demand**, so the context no longer carries choice lists at all. Settings registered in `SettingsManager`: `operatorGate`, `recycleBin`, `traceLog`, `traceHeat`, `traceCountdowns`, `securityAlertLevel`(client), `securityLogSeen`(client). New `SOCKET_OPS`: `TRACE_REQUEST`, `TERMINAL_RESIDENT_REQUEST`. **CSS note:** the tab's prefix is **`ncm-sec-admin-*`, never `ncm-sec-*`** — `ncm-sec-*` belongs to security-overlays.css and colliding with it was the "cards squished to half width, everything centered" bug (see the class-collision gotcha; the `-admin-` infix mirrors `ncm-term-admin-*`). It uses ONLY `--ncm-*` admin tokens (follows all three presets), keeps `box-shadow/text-shadow:none !important` to kill CPR's neon glow, and needs `table { margin: 0 }` (core's `table{margin:1rem}` clipped the last column). Verify with `docs/mockups/admin-security-preview.html` — it renders the **REAL `.hbs`** through Handlebars against real foundry2.css + CPR main.css + admin-theme.css at the true 820px with the scrollbar gutter forced (`?t=clean-light|high-contrast` switches preset, `?diag=1` dumps overflow/wrap/text-align/inheritance measurements, `?empty=1` renders empty states). **Don't hand-copy the markup into the preview** — the original mock did, drifted from the shipped template, and hid all of the above. (Preview note: it `fetch`es the real `.hbs`, so render it with `--allow-file-access-from-files` or the fetch fails; `?h=<px>` grows the frame to reveal lower cards.) **Update round (2026-08-14):** every growable list (log/heat/plants/bin) now caps height + scrolls (`.ncm-sec-admin-scroll`) AND paginates (`_paginate` helper; `_heatPage`/`_plantPage`/`_binPage`; actions `security{Heat,Plant,Bin}Page`). A **Programs card** up top opens the Malware Workshop — Create (`openMalwareWorkshop()`) / Program library (`openMalwareWorkshop({openLibrary:true})`). **Operator Access was redesigned** to radio-CARDS for Anyone/gated (so `gateMode` is a radio group — read `[name=gateMode]:checked`, NOT the first match, in `onSaveGate` + the `.is-ungated` dimming listener), boxed ROLE/SKILL/GEAR blocks that dim when ungated, and **OR-groups with Option A/B headers (`letter` Handlebars helper) + a ✕ remove button** (`securityGateRemoveGroup`; `_renumberGateGroups` re-letters; `_gateGroupInner` keeps the JS-added group in sync with the hbs). Action count is now ~26.

**Shared FUI modals** built alongside: `OperatorSelect.js` (operator picker — see operator-identity section) and the pre-existing `SecurityConsole.js`/`SecurityAuthAdapters.js` seam are the pattern every new modal follows (framework-free promise, appended into `host`, inherits `--ncm-*`).

**Netrunning resolution modes (Thread 7, 2026-07-20) — `hackResolution` A/B/C + the `netAction` tag (design §5).** NCM never pretends to BE netrunning; the table picks HOW gated hack/upload actions resolve. World setting **`hackResolution`** (`auto` default | `flagged` | `gm`), edited in Admin → Security's **Netrunning Resolution** card (auto-saves on change, `[data-sec-hackres]`), with a **per-terminal override** (`terminalConfig.hackResolution`, `'inherit'` default — terminal config → Network tab); precedence via **`resolveHackResolution(item?)`** (helpers.js). The seam is deliberately ONE place: `runSecurityConsole.go()` (plus `BankHeist` phase 2, which owns the siphon roll) — a caller opts in by passing **`gate.net = { verb, action, hostItem?, hostName?, choices? }`**; no `net` = plain Tier A forever (auth/login checks deliberately don't participate — access ≠ action). All the gated content flows are tagged (siphon/crack/unlock/camera/deploy/execute/analyze/exfil/upload/claim).
- **Tier B (`flagged`)** — same auto-roll, then `postNetActionCard` (NetResolve.js) whispers the GM a `.ncm-netcard` naming the NET-action shape + roll + PASS/FAIL, so the table can fold it into a live run.
- **Tier C (`gm`)** — **no roll**: the console parks in an AWAITING GM stage (`is-await`, cancel stays live and **withdraws**) while `requestNetAdjudication` runs a socket round-trip (`SOCKET_OPS.NET_RESOLVE_REQUEST/RESULT/CANCEL`, the exfil userId-filter pattern; GM-as-operator adjudicates locally since emits don't echo; **no GM online → blocked with a warning**; multi-GM → only `game.users.activeGM` prompts). The GM's **NetAdjudicator** (`scripts/ui/content/NetResolve.js` + `styles/components/net-adjudicator.css`, FUI family, prefix `ncm-netadj-*`) shows who/what/verb/DV + the "run the NET at the table, then call it" copy; **DISMISS = withdrawn, not failed** (no trace, retryable; ESC dismisses but a backdrop click deliberately does NOT). The verdict **synthesizes `{ok, total}` so callers are unchanged** — the upload passes ladder `choices` (CLEAN dv+4 / SLOPPY dv−2 / REJECTED dv−BAND−2) whose in-band totals feed `forgeOutcome`/`forgeQualityFromMargin` correctly; synthesized totals are **never displayed as rolls** and never ride the wire.
- **The `netAction` verb is derived centrally**: `NET_ACTION_VERBS` (constants.js) maps the `action` strings already passed to `fireTrace`/`logSuccess` → Backdoor/Control/Virus/Eye-Dee/…; `TraceService._payload` stamps `netVerb` (explicit `o.netVerb` wins, unknown action = none) and the Activity Log renders it as a mono `.nverb` chip. Verified: `test-netresolve.mjs` 42/42 (precedence, protocol, withdraw/late-result/wrong-user, ladder band math) + headless renders of the adjudicator (both verdict sets), the awaiting stage, the Tier B card, and the tab card via `docs/mockups/net-adjudicator-preview.html` (`?ladder=1`, `?view=await|card`, `?snap=1` forces the entrance state for capture — **and the preview MUST mock `Handlebars`**: a missing global makes the modal's Promise-executor throw get swallowed as a silent rejection, i.e. "mounted but never appears").
- **Both sides of the Tier C wait DOCK, and the two are SYNCED (2026-07-22).** The point of `gm` mode is "run the NET at the table first", so neither screen can be held hostage. The GM prompt has a **RUN THE NET** button and the player console/heist a **STEP AWAY** button; both hide the overlay (`is-min`/`is-docked`, click-through) and drop a chip into the shared fixed corner dock **`#ncm-netadj-dock`** (`netadjDock()` exported from NetResolve.js). **When the GM minimizes, the player's console auto-docks in sync** — the dev's ask, "the player shouldn't have to guess the run started": the GM's minimize/restore emits **`SOCKET_OPS.NET_RESOLVE_STATE {requestId, userId, minimized}`** (only for a relayed request — a GM adjudicating their own action has no remote player), `handleNetResolveState` routes it to the `onRemoteState` callback the console passed into `requestNetAdjudication`, and the console docks to a distinct **green "NETRUN IN PROGRESS"** chip (`.ncm-netadj-chip--run`, animated scan bar) vs the cyan self-dock "AWAITING GM" chip. The verdict's arrival auto-undocks; ESC is suppressed while docked. Both SecurityConsole and BankHeist wire it identically (`dock(remote)`/`undock()`). Sim `test-netstate-sync.mjs` 9/9 (real NetResolve: request→state→onRemoteState, wrong-user/unknown/late-STATE dropped, self-adjudication emits no STATE).

**Escalation consequences (2026-07-20) — heat tiers now have teeth (design §6b).** The Admin → Security **Escalate** dialog (GM-gated, one dialog, three options; shown from **warm heat ≥25**, was ≥50) offers **A · Lock out** / **B · Black ICE** / **C · countdown**. The lockout tier's missing map (heat is keyed by bare `objectId`, SecurityService lockouts are keyed granularly) is resolved by **`SecurityTab._lockoutScope(objectId)`** — a terminal fans out to every account key (`termId::acctId`, incl. hidden), a shard → the shard id, a network → the network id (the Agent-connect gate; a terminal's own compound net-join key is deliberately not covered), a `label:` heat → nothing lockable. The primitive is **`SecurityService.forceLockout(actorId, targetId, {durationMs, reason})`** — imposes a lockout immediately without burning attempts (sets attempts→max so login screens read 0 tries left, emits `NETWORK_LOCKOUT{forced:true}`, expires on game time like every other lockout). GM-forced, so it bypasses the account's own `maxAttempts` config. Sim `test-forcelockout.mjs` 18/18. **Still deferred:** the per-terminal **`autoEscalate`** hands-off toggle (all escalation is a manual GM click today) and folding the scaffolded `traceStarted`/`traceExpiresAt` message fields into the countdown.

**Escalation live-test round (2026-07-22) — five fixes off the dev's first Tier-C/escalation playtest:**
- **Tier C must not lock the table out of Foundry — both sides of the wait now DOCK.** The whole point of `gm` mode is "run the NET on the tabletop first", but the adjudicator was a full-screen modal, so the GM couldn't switch scenes/run the netrun before calling it. The GM prompt has a **RUN THE NET** top-bar button and the player-side waits (SecurityConsole `is-await` + BankHeist's siphon wait) a **STEP AWAY** button — all three hide the overlay click-through (`is-min`/`is-docked`) and drop a chip into the shared fixed corner dock **`#ncm-netadj-dock`** (`netadjDock()` exported from NetResolve.js; chip = `.ncm-netadj-chip`, cyan `--wait` variant for the player side). Clicking the chip restores; the verdict's arrival auto-undocks the player console so the outcome animation is never missed; **ESC is suppressed while docked** (a stray keypress must not silently withdraw a run in progress) and the promise flow is untouched — withdraw/dismiss still clean up the chip.
- **Forced lockout must SYNC — SecurityService state is CLIENT-LOCAL.** The escalation `forceLockout` only wrote the GM's in-memory Map, so the locked player just logged back in, and the per-account event fan-out spammed the GM with N "Unknown — ? min" toasts. Now **`forceLockoutMany(actorId, targets, {durationMs, reason, label})`** is the escalation entry: applies locally, emits ONE forced `NETWORK_LOCKOUT`, broadcasts **`SOCKET_OPS.SECURITY_LOCKOUT`** → every client mirrors via `receiveForcedLockout` (state + timers, no re-broadcast), **kicks live sessions** (`_kickLockedViewers` scans ui.windows + foundry.applications.instances; a terminal viewer gets the new **`TerminalViewerApp.forceLogout(msg)`** → bounced to login with the error line, a shard viewer closes), and the owning non-GM player gets ONE labeled toast + `lockout` sound. `NotificationService`'s `NETWORK_LOCKOUT` listener **skips `forced` events** entirely, and `showNetworkLockout` now resolves the target label for ALL key shapes via `_lockTargetLabel` (network id / shard item / `termId::acctId` → "Item · Account") + minutes from `duration` — the "lock unknown … ? min" fix for earned lockouts too. Sim: scratchpad `test-lockout-sync.mjs` (23/23, real SecurityService, two simulated clients).
- **Countdowns are visible and have TEETH.** The reaper was registered but a started countdown was invisible ("I did countdown and nothing happened" — also note it runs on **game time**, so manual/world-time clocks stall it). The Security tab's Heat card now lists **active countdowns** (`_gatherCountdowns` — remaining time, a clock icon + tooltip when a non-real-time provider gates it, **Call off** button → `securityCountdownCancel`), and `reapCountdowns` expiry additionally writes a durable **`trace-complete`** Activity Log row at `hostile` severity (+35 heat → typically BURNED, which also fires the GM alert toast) on top of the existing whispers + sound.
- **Black ICE toast reaches the player.** `ICEService.applyDamage`'s toast rode the client-local EventBus, so a GM escalation strike showed only on the GM's screen — and the listener resolved the name from a nonexistent `data.itemId` ("'Unknown' fights back"). The listener now prefers `iceInfo.name`/`targetName`, and applyDamage relays **`SOCKET_OPS.SECURITY_NOTIFY`** `{userIds, kind:'blackIce', data}` to the actor's active non-GM owners (handler shows the toast + `black-ice` sound). SECURITY_NOTIFY is a generic targeted-toast op — reuse it for future cross-client security pings.
- **The Security tab live-updates.** The log/heat/countdowns/bin are WORLD SETTINGS, so `AdminPanelApp._setupEventSubscriptions` registers an **`updateSetting` hook** (filtered to `traceLog`/`traceHeat`/`traceCountdowns`/`recycleBin`, debounced, security-tab-only; `Hooks.off` in `close()`) — it fires on every client including the writer. A 30s `_liveTick` in `SecurityTab.onRender` (cleared in `onClose`) repaints heat **decay** and countdown remaining-times, which change without any setting write. The panel was already `minimizable: true` — double-click the title bar.

**Second escalation-notes round (2026-07-22b) — legibility + copy + fire default:**
- **Fire default flipped to AUTO.** `residentFireMode` default is now `'auto'` (fires + tells the GM) instead of `'propose'`; `TerminalService._proposeResidentFire`'s local fallback matches. The prompt/pause is now opt-in.
- **Trace countdowns aren't always "the corp".** `startCountdown` gained a free-text **`source`** ("who's closing in" — Arasaka / a fixer / the Maelstrom, default "A trace") and **`playerWarning`** (custom player cue); the Escalate → countdown dialog surfaces both (Who / Warn fields), the Security-tab countdown list shows the source (`.cdsrc`), and all countdown whispers name it. The old hardcoded "the corp found you" is gone.
- **Player-facing trace warnings are legible cards now.** New shared helper **`whisperCardHtml({tone, icon, title, lines})`** (helpers.js) + **`.ncm-wcard`** (styles/chat/chat-cards.css §9, hard fallbacks — chat log has no `--ncm-*` ancestor; tones info/warn/danger/ok/purple). Every bare `<b>NCM trace:</b> …` whisper (heat adjust, trace start/complete, lockout, ICE) — in TraceService AND SecurityTab — routes through it at ~13.5px body vs Foundry's default. Reuse it for any future NCM security whisper.
- **Toast X + countdown made visible.** `.ncm-toast__dismiss` was a 7px near-black glyph hidden until hover → now 11px, always at 0.65 opacity. `.ncm-toast__progress` was a 2px/0.35-opacity bar → 3px, 0.85 opacity + glow over a dark track. (Applies to every toast, not just security.)
- **ICE retaliation card — legibility + context.** Chat-card `.ncm-cc__result-badge` (SUCCESS/FAILED) bumped 8→11px and `.ncm-cc__roll-label` 7→9px (the "success/fail can be more legible" + "roll labels" notes). The ICE card (`.ncm-ice-card__*`) got a size pass (badge 9→11, name 13→15, portrait 56→68px + a red **scan-sweep** over the pulled-in image, damage number 30→36 with a pulse, formula/HP/footer up ~1–2px). **Its title is context-aware**: `showShardBlackICE` reads `data.context` → "BLACK ICE — Shard/Network/Message/Terminal" (was hardcoded "— Shard"; a trace-escalation strike said the wrong thing). `context` now threads BLACK_ICE_DAMAGE event → NotificationService AND the SECURITY_NOTIFY relay. `.ncm-netcard` (Tier B whisper) fonts bumped too. Verified: headless `legibility-preview.html` render (result badge, roll grid, ICE-with-Network-context, four whisper cards — all legible).

**Third escalation-notes round (2026-07-22c) — lockout hardening + visibility + dialog polish:**
- **THE lockout re-login bug (critical): `requiresAuth` let a locked-out player straight back in TWO ways** — an OPEN account (no auth) and a session that still "remembered" the login both returned `requiresAuth=false` and dropped into `_loginSuccess` with NO lockout check. Fixed with **`TerminalViewerApp._lockGuard(accountId)`** — the authoritative gate: it reads `TerminalService.getAccountLockoutRemaining` (new, → `SecurityService.getLockoutRemaining`) and runs FIRST on every login entry point (`_onSelectAccount`/`_onFocusAccount`/`_onInlineAuth`/`_onSwitchAccount`) AND as the final gate inside `_loginSuccess` (so no path — open account, remembered session, keypad — reaches the mailbox past an active lockout). `_cardContext` now exposes `lockedOut`; the login card renders a red **LOCKED OUT** badge (`.is-lockedout`) and clicking it re-shows the takeover.
- **SYSTEM LOCKDOWN takeover (the "animation of what's happening").** `_syncLockdown()` (called in `_onRender`, GM-excluded) injects a full-viewer red alert overlay (`.ncm-term__lockdown` in terminal.css — hazard stripes, sweeping scan bar, pulsing lock, a **live MM:SS countdown** on a 1s interval, STAND DOWN dismiss) whenever `_lockdownAccountId` is set and still locked. `forceLogout` sets it (keyed on the kicked account); when the clock hits 0 it clears + re-renders so the cards go clickable. Timer cleared in `close()`. Reduced-motion drops the animations.
- **Player-facing countdown HUD (the "I don't see anything on the player side" fix).** New **`scripts/ui/content/TraceCountdownHud.js`** (registered ready/43 on EVERY client next to TraceService; `game.nightcity.traceCountdownHud`) — a body-mounted top-right panel (`#ncm-trace-hud` in net-adjudicator.css) that shows every active `traceCountdowns` entry targeting an actor THIS user owns: WHO is closing in, on WHAT, a live MM:SS ticking on the **game clock** (same basis as the GM reaper, so they never disagree). Rebuilds on the `traceCountdowns` `updateSetting` hook, entrance-flashes new ones, self-clears when empty. Reads the world-scope setting directly — no socket. A GM normally sees nothing (they don't own the targeted PC).
- **Escalate dialog stylized.** Was a raw grey Foundry `Dialog` of inline-styled rows; now three labeled **option cards** (A/B/C tag badges, WARM+/COUNTDOWN chips, per-option accent) + a red danger lede + a reason/warn-player footer, with FUI-vocabulary buttons (lockout amber, countdown/ICE red). Passed `{classes:['ncm-esc-dialog'], width:440}`; styles are **top-level** in tab-security.css (a Dialog is body-mounted, NOT inside `.ncm-admin-panel`, so they can't use the `-admin-` scope) with hard fallbacks. Verified headless (`escalate-preview.html`, `lockdown-preview.html`).

**Still deferred (next session):** Tier B/C **program-item hooks** (equipped Sword/Worm modifying DVs — §5's "later hook"), `autoEscalate` toggle, plus the standing polish list.

**Thread 6 — EXECUTE malware verbs + SYSTEM SWEEP + malware-uploadable (2026-07-24→25, BUILT, sim/headless-verified, NOT live-tested).** The offensive half of the player→machine arc. Decisions locked in `docs/THREAD6-MALWARE-MODEL.md` (GM-authored economy w/ store/Item-Piles selling; 24h backdoors; deployICE both-sources; timed-single logicBomb; worm out). Design: `docs/UPLOAD-FORGE-TRACE.md` §4e. **The five stubbed resident verbs are now native**, grown into the TWO existing appliers (no third added):
- **Triggered verbs** live in `TerminalService.fireResident`, which was split into a reusable **`_fireResidentEffect(item, effectType, params, ctx)`** switch (so `logicBomb` can run an INNER effect without re-marking the outer resident). **`keylog`** captures the login that fires it → whispers the account (a secret-login lead) to the runner+GM via `_deliverToRunner`. **`forgeAccount`** → **`forgeBackdoorAccount`** (shared with the instant path): writes a HIDDEN, password-protected account with a `forged` provenance block + a 24h game-time expiry (`reapForgedAccounts` on the resident-reaper cadence closes it, `ttlHours:0`=permanent); creds whisper to the runner. **`deployICE`** bites the intruder whose login fired it — runner-brought `params.iceActorId` wins, else `_resolveDeployIce` reads the box's own account `security.ice`, else honest degrade. **`logicBomb`** runs ONE inner effect through the same switch; an inner `logicBomb` is refused (the recursion fence).
- **Instant verbs** live in SiphonFlow's **`_applyNativeEffect`** (now EXPORTED for sims, where `wipe` lives): **`corrupt`** redacts document/dossier paragraphs/sections IN PLACE + arms a DECLASSIFY (DV) to restore — the reversible sabotage; non-redactable types are honestly skipped (`_corruptContentData`). **`forgeAccount`** (instant variant) delegates to `forgeBackdoorAccount`.
- **SYSTEM SWEEP** (`scripts/ui/content/SystemSweep.js` + `styles/components/system-sweep.css`) — the counterplay: a cyan FUI-family promise modal (the defensive sibling of UploadConsole; radar-sweep beat) wired into `TerminalViewerApp`'s OPERATOR-TOOLS dropdown (`terminalSystemSweep`→`_onSystemSweep`). ONE sweep roll (`performCheck`, Interface, card DV 15) → reveals every ARMED resident whose `stealthDV ≤ total` (GM-without-actor force-reveals); the verdict **never says how many you missed**. **Information split:** GM sees type+`provenance`+`stealthDV`+forgery-tell (forgeQuality<45=SLOPPY else WELL-MADE); a player sees only "UNKNOWN PROCESS"+a vague per-type hint. Per-resident **DEFUSE** → `updateResident(state:'defused')` (GM direct / player relays via **`TERMINAL_RESIDENT_REQUEST {op:'defuse', residentId}`** — `handleResidentRequest` branches on `data.op`). No trace/heat (recon). Mock `docs/mockups/system-sweep-mock.html` (`?view=player|gm`, `?freeze=scan`); real CSS verified through the full import chain (cyan from content-types.css `[data-ct-color]`).
- **Malware-uploadable authoring** — the composer's uploadable fieldset (`data-shard-composer.hbs` + `DataShardComposer` context/collect) gains a **"When pushed, it…" verb select** (`uploadEffect`: keylog/backdoor/deployICE) that renders ONLY on malware/payload (context `isExecuteProgram`); labels flip Forge→Run. `TerminalViewerApp._gatherCarriedPrograms` branches: malware/payload+`uploadEffect` → an EXECUTE program (`effectType`=verb, `family:'execute'`, `params.iceActorId` for deployICE from the malware form's linked ICE) — the old `disabled:'execute'` SOON gate is GONE; anything else uploads as a PLANT (incl. a verb-less malware file). `runUpload` reuses the SAME three-band forge LADDER + generic installer — only console/notify COPY is family-aware (`isExecute`: FORGE→RUN, "install/glitchy/rejected"). So an uploaded EXECUTE program installs a triggered resident (fires onLogin), caught by SWEEP.
- **Deliberately NOT built** (deferred, not forgotten): `runProgram` unification (a pure refactor of two *working* flows — `runPayloadExecute`/`runMalwareDeploy` — that the upload path never routes through; no user-facing benefit, regression risk); composer authoring of `logicBomb`'s inner effect; `corrupt`/`wipe` as *uploadable* verbs (they need push-time target-file selection).
- **Test macro:** `tools/test-thread6-setup.macro.js` seeds a Black-ICE actor + an operator-owned go-bag shard (3 EXECUTE uploadables: keylog/backdoor/deployICE + 1 PLANT for contrast) + **pre-armed residents at four stealth DVs** on the first terminal, so SYSTEM SWEEP has targets on the first run. (The `plant`-only `tools/test-upload-setup.macro.js` predates this — its "EXECUTE is SOON" note is now stale.)

## Architecture: the Shard Encoder (player-facing authoring) + the legibility layer

The player's answer to the GM's Malware Workshop: a blank chip → something on it → one roll. Design is signed off (the seven decisions are in the `shard-encoder-design` memory note); the approved mock is `docs/mockups/shard-encoder-fui-mock.html`. **Built 2026-09-21: BLANK → BUILD (content **or** program) → LOCK → ENCODE, plus the GM card, the accessibility layer and the Market.** Nothing of the flow is left stubbed; what remains is retiring the old wizard and a live test.

**`STAGES` is the ONE list** the spine, the footer count and the navigation read, and its **middle row is a BRANCH**: the same slot is CONTENT (forge a file) or PROGRAM (build a tool), chosen by `S.mode`, which is why its `title`/`sub` are `{content, program}` maps read through `stageText()`. A new stage slots in by adding a row plus a `_rail<Stage>()` / `_patch<Stage>()` pair. Entry defaults to CONTENT; the **Payload / Malware tiles switch the branch** (the mock's "② CONTENT / ② PROGRAM"), and the Program Deck's door asks for `program` by name.

**The rules are a pure module — `scripts/ui/content/EncoderModel.js` — and it is the ONLY place a number lives.** No Foundry globals, so `tools/sims/test-encoder-model.mjs` (49 checks) drives the real functions. `ENCODER_DEFAULTS` is the shape; the world setting **`encoderConfig` stores ONLY what the GM changed** and `resolveEncoderConfig(stored)` layers it over the defaults (objects merge, **arrays replace**, so a deleted tier stays deleted) — which is why a default added in a later version reaches existing worlds **with no migration**. It also sanitises: an unknown botch rule / perk / pricing mode falls back, a negative price clamps, a nameless item entry is dropped, and there is **always exactly one fallback tier**.
